<script>
window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {

  // Add body.loaded for play button fade-in (200ms delay on mobile to prevent flicker)
  if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
    setTimeout(() => document.body.classList.add('loaded'), 200);
  } else {
    document.body.classList.add('loaded');
  }

  // ── HERO: Fetch Vimeo thumbnails (commented out — hero uses CMS cover image instead)
  // document.querySelectorAll('.video-wrapper.vimeo').forEach(wrapper => {
  //   const id = wrapper.dataset.videoId;
  //   if (!id) return;
  //   fetch(`https://vimeo.com/api/oembed.json?url=https://vimeo.com/${id}`)
  //     .then(r => r.json())
  //     .then(data => {
  //       const thumb = wrapper.querySelector('.vimeo-thumb');
  //       if (thumb && data.thumbnail_url) {
  //         thumb.src = data.thumbnail_url.replace(/-d_\d+x\d+/, '-d_1280x720');
  //       }
  //     })
  //     .catch(() => {});
  // });

  // ── HERO: Click-to-play (YouTube + Vimeo) ────────────────────────────────
  document.querySelectorAll('.video-wrapper').forEach(wrapper => {
    const id = wrapper.dataset.videoId;
    if (!id) return;

    const isVimeo = wrapper.classList.contains('vimeo');
    wrapper.style.cursor = 'pointer';

    wrapper.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const src = isVimeo
        ? `https://player.vimeo.com/video/${id}?autoplay=1&muted=0`
        : `https://www.youtube.com/embed/${id}?autoplay=1&rel=0&modestbranding=1&showinfo=0&controls=1`;

      const rect = wrapper.getBoundingClientRect();
      wrapper.style.width    = rect.width + 'px';
      wrapper.style.height   = rect.height + 'px';
      wrapper.style.position = 'relative';

      const playBtn = wrapper.querySelector('.play-button');
      if (playBtn) playBtn.style.display = 'none';

      const iframe = document.createElement('iframe');
      iframe.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;top:0;left:0;width:100%;height:100%;border:none;z-index:1;';
      iframe.setAttribute('allow', 'accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; fullscreen; web-share');
      iframe.setAttribute('referrerpolicy', 'strict-origin-when-cross-origin');
      iframe.setAttribute('allowfullscreen', '');

      const thumb = wrapper.querySelector('.video-thumbnail');
      if (thumb) thumb.style.cssText += ';position:relative;z-index:2;';

      iframe.onload = () => { if (thumb) thumb.remove(); };
      iframe.src = src;
      wrapper.appendChild(iframe);

    }, { once: true });
  });

  // ── RICH TEXT: Vimeo autoplay embeds ─────────────────────────────────────
  document.querySelectorAll('.vimeo-embed-wrapper').forEach(wrapper => {
    const id     = wrapper.dataset.vimeoId;
    const img    = wrapper.querySelector('.vimeo-poster');
    const iframe = wrapper.querySelector('iframe');

    wrapper.style.cssText = 'position:relative;padding-bottom:56.25%;height:0;overflow:hidden;background:#000;';
    img.style.cssText     = 'position:absolute;top:0;left:0;width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;z-index:1;transition:opacity 0.3s ease;background:#000;';
    iframe.style.cssText  = 'position:absolute;top:0;left:0;width:100%;height:100%;border:none;z-index:2;';

    img.src = `https://vumbnail.com/${id}.jpg`;

    fetch(`https://vimeo.com/api/oembed.json?url=https://vimeo.com/${id}`)
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(data => { img.alt = data.title || 'Vimeo video preview'; })
      .catch(() => { img.alt = 'Vimeo video preview'; });

    iframe.src = `https://player.vimeo.com/video/${id}?background=1&autoplay=1&loop=1&muted=1&byline=0&title=0&controls=0`;
    iframe.onload = () => { img.style.opacity = '0'; };
  });

});
</script>
<!-- ✅ END: Blog post video scripts -->






<!-- ✅ START: Theme on Scroll + Navbar Sync -->
<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  /* Controls theme variable switching when .theme-trigger crosses the trigger line.
     Updated to support both the new navbar structure and old navbar fallbacks. */

  const triggerEls = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.theme-trigger'));

  /* --- Navbar target: new classes first, old fallbacks after --- */
  const navbarEl =
    document.querySelector('.navbar-ltx') ||
    document.querySelector('.navbar-new-component') ||
    document.querySelector('.navbar12_component') ||
    document.querySelector('.navbar-ltx.cc-glass') ||
    document.querySelector('.navbar-ltx2') ||
    document.querySelector('.navbar') ||
    document.querySelector('nav');

  const refBase  = document.querySelector('.theme-ref-base');
  const refLight = document.querySelector('.theme-ref-light');

  if (triggerEls.length === 0 || !refBase || !refLight) {
    console.error('Theme toggle: Missing required elements', {
      triggerElsCount: triggerEls.length,
      navbarEl,
      refBase,
      refLight
    });
    return;
  }

  const THEME_PREFIX = '--_theme';

  /* --- Child targets inside navbar / nearby sections --- */
  const CHILD_THEME_TARGET_SELECTOR =
    '.ltx-but-base:not(.ltx-but-special):not(.is-special):not([data-theme-exclude="true"]), ' +
    '.ltx-but-flex:not(.ltx-but-special):not(.is-special):not([data-theme-exclude="true"]), ' +
    '.ltx-but-white-small:not(.ltx-but-special):not(.is-special):not([data-theme-exclude="true"])';

  function readThemeVars(refEl) {
    const styles = getComputedStyle(refEl);
    const vars = {};

    for (let i = 0; i < styles.length; i++) {
      const prop = styles[i];

      if (prop.startsWith(THEME_PREFIX)) {
        vars[prop] = styles.getPropertyValue(prop).trim();
      }
    }

    return vars;
  }

  function applyThemeVars(targetEl, vars) {
    Object.entries(vars).forEach(([name, value]) => {
      targetEl.style.setProperty(name, value);
    });
  }

  function clearThemeVars(targetEl, varNames) {
    varNames.forEach(name => targetEl.style.removeProperty(name));
  }

  const baseVars  = readThemeVars(refBase);
  const lightVars = readThemeVars(refLight);

  const allThemeVarNames = Array.from(
    new Set([...Object.keys(baseVars), ...Object.keys(lightVars)])
  );

  const navbarTargets = navbarEl ? [navbarEl] : [];

  function getNeighborTargets(triggerEl) {
    const prev1 = triggerEl.previousElementSibling;
    const prev2 = prev1 ? prev1.previousElementSibling : null;

    const next1 = triggerEl.nextElementSibling;
    const next2 = next1 ? next1.nextElementSibling : null;

    return [prev2, prev1, next1, next2].filter(Boolean);
  }

  function getTargetsForTrigger(triggerEl) {
    const baseTargets = [...getNeighborTargets(triggerEl), ...navbarTargets];

    const childTargets = baseTargets.flatMap(el => {
      if (!el) return [];
      return Array.from(el.querySelectorAll(CHILD_THEME_TARGET_SELECTOR));
    });

    return Array.from(new Set([...baseTargets, ...childTargets]));
  }

  function applyThemeToTriggerTargets(triggerEl, vars) {
    getTargetsForTrigger(triggerEl).forEach(el => {
      applyThemeVars(el, vars);
    });
  }

  function clearAndApplyBaseForTrigger(triggerEl) {
    getTargetsForTrigger(triggerEl).forEach(el => {
      clearThemeVars(el, allThemeVarNames);
      applyThemeVars(el, baseVars);
    });
  }

  const currentThemeByTrigger = new WeakMap();
  const prevTopByTrigger = new WeakMap();

  function setLight(triggerEl) {
    if (currentThemeByTrigger.get(triggerEl) === 'light') return;

    currentThemeByTrigger.set(triggerEl, 'light');
    applyThemeToTriggerTargets(triggerEl, lightVars);
  }

  function setBase(triggerEl) {
    if (currentThemeByTrigger.get(triggerEl) === 'base') return;

    currentThemeByTrigger.set(triggerEl, 'base');
    clearAndApplyBaseForTrigger(triggerEl);
  }

  /* --- Trigger position --- */
  const TRIGGER_POINT_DESKTOP = 0.1; /* 10% from the top */
  const TRIGGER_POINT_MOBILE  = 0.1; /* 10% from the top */
  const MOBILE_MAX_WIDTH = 767;

  function getTriggerPoint() {
    return window.innerWidth <= MOBILE_MAX_WIDTH
      ? TRIGGER_POINT_MOBILE
      : TRIGGER_POINT_DESKTOP;
  }

  function getTriggerLineY() {
    return window.innerHeight * getTriggerPoint();
  }

  let ticking = false;

  function checkCrossing() {
    ticking = false;

    const lineY = getTriggerLineY();

    triggerEls.forEach(triggerEl => {
      const top = triggerEl.getBoundingClientRect().top;
      const prevTop = prevTopByTrigger.get(triggerEl);

      if (prevTop === null || prevTop === undefined) {
        prevTopByTrigger.set(triggerEl, top);
        return;
      }

      if (prevTop > lineY && top <= lineY) setLight(triggerEl);
      if (prevTop < lineY && top >= lineY) setBase(triggerEl);

      prevTopByTrigger.set(triggerEl, top);
    });
  }

  function onScroll() {
    if (ticking) return;

    ticking = true;
    requestAnimationFrame(checkCrossing);
  }

  window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true });

  window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
    triggerEls.forEach(triggerEl => {
      prevTopByTrigger.set(triggerEl, null);
    });

    onScroll();
  });

  /* --- Initial state --- */
  triggerEls.forEach(triggerEl => {
    currentThemeByTrigger.set(triggerEl, 'base');
    prevTopByTrigger.set(triggerEl, null);
    clearAndApplyBaseForTrigger(triggerEl);
  });

  onScroll();
});
</script>
<!-- ✅ END: Theme on Scroll + Navbar Sync -->

data-optanongroupid="C0004"><div class="category-menu-switch-handler" role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="ot-desc-id-C0004"><h3 id="ot-header-id-C0004">Targeting id="ot-pvcy-hdr">Cookie Preferences</h4><p id="ot-pc-desc" class="ot-grp-desc">When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. Click on the different category headings to find out more and change our default settings. However, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience of the site and the services we are able to offer.
            <br><a href="https://static.facetuneapp.com/legal/cookie-policy.pdf" class="privacy-notice-link" rel="noopener" target="_blank" aria-label="More information about your privacy, opens in a new tab">More information</a><button class="ot-link-btn onetrust-vendors-list-handler" aria-label="Cookies details">
                    Cookies details
                    </button></p></div><div class="ot-desc-cntr ot-hide" role="tabpanel" tabindex="0" id="ot-desc-id-C0003"><div class="ot-grp-hdr1"><h4 class="ot-cat-header">Functional Cookies</h4><div class="ot-tgl"><input type="checkbox" name="ot-group-id-C0003" id="ot-group-id-C0003" class="category-switch-handler" data-optanongroupid="C0003" checked="" aria-labelledby="ot-header-id-C0003"> <label class="ot-switch" for="ot-group-id-C0003"><span class="ot-switch-nob"></span> <span class="ot-label-txt">Functional Cookies</span></label> </div><div class="ot-tgl-cntr"></div></div><p class="ot-grp-desc ot-category-desc">These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.</p><div class="ot-hlst-cntr"><button class="ot-link-btn category-host-list-handler" aria-label="Functional Cookies - Cookie Details button opens Cookie List menu" data-parent-id="C0003">Cookies Details‎</button></div></div><div class="ot-desc-cntr ot-hide ot-always-active-group" role="tabpanel" tabindex="0" id="ot-desc-id-C0001"><div class="ot-grp-hdr1"><h4 class="ot-cat-header">Strictly Necessary Cookies</h4><div class="ot-tgl-cntr"><div class="ot-always-active">Always Active</div></div></div><p class="ot-grp-desc ot-category-desc">These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.</p><div class="ot-hlst-cntr"><button class="ot-link-btn category-host-list-handler" aria-label="Strictly Necessary Cookies - Cookie Details button opens Cookie List menu" data-parent-id="C0001">Cookies Details‎</button></div></div><div class="ot-desc-cntr ot-hide" role="tabpanel" tabindex="0" id="ot-desc-id-C0002"><div class="ot-grp-hdr1"><h4 class="ot-cat-header">Performance Cookies</h4><div class="ot-tgl"><input type="checkbox" name="ot-group-id-C0002" id="ot-group-id-C0002" class="category-switch-handler" data-optanongroupid="C0002" checked="" aria-labelledby="ot-header-id-C0002"> <label class="ot-switch" for="ot-group-id-C0002"><span class="ot-switch-nob"></span> <span class="ot-label-txt">Performance Cookies</span></label> </div><div class="ot-tgl-cntr"></div></div><p class="ot-grp-desc ot-category-desc">These cookies allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. All information these cookies collect is aggregated and therefore anonymous. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site, and will not be able to monitor its performance.</p><div class="ot-hlst-cntr"><button class="ot-link-btn category-host-list-handler" aria-label="Performance Cookies - Cookie Details button opens Cookie List menu" data-parent-id="C0002">Cookies Details‎</button></div></div><div class="ot-desc-cntr ot-hide" role="tabpanel" tabindex="0" id="ot-desc-id-C0004"><div class="ot-grp-hdr1"><h4 class="ot-cat-header">Targeting Cookies</h4><div class="ot-tgl"><input type="checkbox" name="ot-group-id-C0004" id="ot-group-id-C0004" class="category-switch-handler" data-optanongroupid="C0004" checked="" aria-labelledby="ot-header-id-C0004"> <label class="ot-switch" for="ot-group-id-C0004"><span class="ot-switch-nob"></span> <span class="ot-label-txt">Targeting Cookies</span></label> </div><div class="ot-tgl-cntr"></div></div><p class="ot-grp-desc ot-category-desc">These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.</p><div class="ot-hlst-cntr"><button class="ot-link-btn category-host-list-handler" aria-label="Targeting Cookies - Cookie Details button opens Cookie List menu" data-parent-id="C0004">Cookies Details‎</button></div></div></div></div></div><!-- Vendors / Hosts --><section id="ot-pc-lst" class="ot-hide ot-pc-scrollbar ot-enbl-chr"><div class="ot-lst-cntr ot-pc-scrollbar"><div id="ot-pc-hdr"><div id="ot-lst-title"><button class="ot-link-btn back-btn-handler" aria-label="Back"><svg id="ot-back-arw" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" x="0px" y="0px" viewBox="0 0 444.531 444.531" xml:space="preserve"><title>Back Button</title><g><path fill="#656565" d="M213.13,222.409L351.88,83.653c7.05-7.043,10.567-15.657,10.567-25.841c0-10.183-3.518-18.793-10.567-25.835

id="ot-pvcy-hdr">Cookie Preferences</h4><p id="ot-pc-desc" class="ot-grp-desc">When you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences or your device and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect it to. The information does not usually directly identify you, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. Click on the different category headings to find out more and change our default settings. However, blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience of the site and the services we are able to offer.
            <br><a href="https://static.facetuneapp.com/legal/cookie-policy.pdf" class="privacy-notice-link" rel="noopener" target="_blank" aria-label="More information about your privacy, opens in a new tab">More information</a><button class="ot-link-btn onetrust-vendors-list-handler" aria-label="Cookies details">
                    Cookies details
                    </button></p></div><div class="ot-desc-cntr ot-hide" role="tabpanel" tabindex="0" id="ot-desc-id-C0003"><div class="ot-grp-hdr1"><h4 class="ot-cat-header">Functional Cookies</h4><div class="ot-tgl"><input type="checkbox" name="ot-group-id-C0003" id="ot-group-id-C0003" class="category-switch-handler" data-optanongroupid="C0003" checked="" aria-labelledby="ot-header-id-C0003"> <label class="ot-switch" for="ot-group-id-C0003"><span class="ot-switch-nob"></span> <span class="ot-label-txt">Functional Cookies</span></label> </div><div class="ot-tgl-cntr"></div></div><p class="ot-grp-desc ot-category-desc">These cookies enable the website to provide enhanced functionality and personalisation. They may be set by us or by third party providers whose services we have added to our pages. If you do not allow these cookies then some or all of these services may not function properly.</p><div class="ot-hlst-cntr"><button class="ot-link-btn category-host-list-handler" aria-label="Functional Cookies - Cookie Details button opens Cookie List menu" data-parent-id="C0003">Cookies Details‎</button></div></div><div class="ot-desc-cntr ot-hide ot-always-active-group" role="tabpanel" tabindex="0" id="ot-desc-id-C0001"><div class="ot-grp-hdr1"><h4 class="ot-cat-header">Strictly Necessary Cookies</h4><div class="ot-tgl-cntr"><div class="ot-always-active">Always Active</div></div></div><p class="ot-grp-desc ot-category-desc">These cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.</p><div class="ot-hlst-cntr"><button class="ot-link-btn category-host-list-handler" aria-label="Strictly Necessary Cookies - Cookie Details button opens Cookie List menu" data-parent-id="C0001">Cookies Details‎</button></div></div><div class="ot-desc-cntr ot-hide" role="tabpanel" tabindex="0" id="ot-desc-id-C0002"><div class="ot-grp-hdr1"><h4 class="ot-cat-header">Performance Cookies</h4><div class="ot-tgl"><input type="checkbox" name="ot-group-id-C0002" id="ot-group-id-C0002" class="category-switch-handler" data-optanongroupid="C0002" checked="" aria-labelledby="ot-header-id-C0002"> <label class="ot-switch" for="ot-group-id-C0002"><span class="ot-switch-nob"></span> <span class="ot-label-txt">Performance Cookies</span></label> </div><div class="ot-tgl-cntr"></div></div><p class="ot-grp-desc ot-category-desc">These cookies allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. All information these cookies collect is aggregated and therefore anonymous. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site, and will not be able to monitor its performance.</p><div class="ot-hlst-cntr"><button class="ot-link-btn category-host-list-handler" aria-label="Performance Cookies - Cookie Details button opens Cookie List menu" data-parent-id="C0002">Cookies Details‎</button></div></div><div class="ot-desc-cntr ot-hide" role="tabpanel" tabindex="0" id="ot-desc-id-C0004"><div class="ot-grp-hdr1"><h4 class="ot-cat-header">Targeting Cookies</h4><div class="ot-tgl"><input type="checkbox" name="ot-group-id-C0004" id="ot-group-id-C0004" class="category-switch-handler" data-optanongroupid="C0004" checked="" aria-labelledby="ot-header-id-C0004"> <label class="ot-switch" for="ot-group-id-C0004"><span class="ot-switch-nob"></span> <span class="ot-label-txt">Targeting Cookies</span></label> </div><div class="ot-tgl-cntr"></div></div><p class="ot-grp-desc ot-category-desc">These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.</p><div class="ot-hlst-cntr"><button class="ot-link-btn category-host-list-handler" aria-label="Targeting Cookies - Cookie Details button opens Cookie List menu" data-parent-id="C0004">Cookies Details‎</button></div></div></div></div></div><!-- Vendors / Hosts --><section id="ot-pc-lst" class="ot-hide ot-pc-scrollbar ot-enbl-chr"><div class="ot-lst-cntr ot-pc-scrollbar"><div id="ot-pc-hdr"><div id="ot-lst-title"><button class="ot-link-btn back-btn-handler" aria-label="Back"><svg id="ot-back-arw" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" x="0px" y="0px" viewBox="0 0 444.531 444.531" xml:space="preserve"><title>Back Button</title><g><path fill="#656565" d="M213.13,222.409L351.88,83.653c7.05-7.043,10.567-15.657,10.567-25.841c0-10.183-3.518-18.793-10.567-25.835
